# Agents · R · part 8

937 entries, research-discovery to researcher. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **research-discovery**: Surface net-new feature ideas from outside the existing roadmap — competitive landscape, platform capabilities, behavior research, App Store / Reddit signal. Conversational loop with a final gate before filing each surviving idea as a `research-proposal`-labeled Feature issue. Use when the human wants to expand the idea pool, periodically scan for opportunities, or dive on a specific topic. Do NOT use for: ranking existing roadmap items (use `feature-impact-ranking`), fleshing out a known idea into a spec (use `spec-writer`), or evaluating already-filed proposals (use `evaluator`). ([BoutLabs/cornerman-claude-plugin](https://github.com/BoutLabs/cornerman-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/research-discovery.md))
- **research-dispatcher**: Decides whether deep research is needed for a task, checks the research cache, and dispatches to deep-researcher. Returns cached brief if fresh (&lt; 24h). ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/research-dispatcher.md) · ★21)
- **research-distiller**: Converts messy notes and raw research into concise, linked evergreen notes. ([gupta-builds/second-brain-claudekit](https://github.com/gupta-builds/second-brain-claudekit/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-distiller.md))
- **research-diver**: You are a research deep-dive agent. Your job is to thoroughly analyze a cluster of related findings from the scout phase, fetch and study the source material, and write a standalone synthesis report. ([JustinCooper-C3/claudius](https://github.com/JustinCooper-C3/claudius/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-diver.md))
- **research-doc-formatter**: Specialist in formatting documents, creating mind maps, and structuring workflows for clarity and ease of understanding. ([datagodzilla/ClinPort](https://github.com/datagodzilla/ClinPort/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebooklm-doc-formatter.md))
- **research-doc-generator**: Documentation generation specialist that creates comprehensive, well-formatted documentation using formatting best practices. Serves other agents with professional documentation output. ([datagodzilla/ClinPort](https://github.com/datagodzilla/ClinPort/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebooklm-doc-generator.md))
- **research-doc-parser**: Document parser for reading and understanding documents on AI and clinical topics. Extracts all information and outputs to text file for other agents. ([datagodzilla/ClinPort](https://github.com/datagodzilla/ClinPort/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/notebooklm-doc-parser.md))
- **research-docs**: Documentation researcher. Fetches and reads upstream docs for Rust crates, Perl modules, LSP/DAP protocols, and tooling. Returns condensed API references and usage examples. Use when agents need to verify API signatures, protocol compliance, or library behavior. ([EffortlessMetrics/perl-lsp](https://github.com/EffortlessMetrics/perl-lsp/blob/HEAD/docs/handoff/swarm-pack/agents/research-docs.md) · ★27 · 2 copies)
- **research-dossier-writer**: Creates a PRP-style research dossier for a feature brief, focused on concrete codebase anchors, patterns, gotchas, and supporting docs. Used by create-plan as a supporting artifact before reconciliation. ([dcouple/Pane](https://github.com/dcouple/Pane/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-dossier-writer.md) · ★474)
- **research-ecosystem**: Researches Claude Code ecosystem from official Anthropic documentation. Dispatched by /claudit during Phase 1. Builds expert knowledge on MCP, plugins, hooks, skills, and subagents. ([acostanzo/quickstop](https://github.com/acostanzo/quickstop/blob/HEAD/plugins/claudit/agents/research-ecosystem.md) · ★46)
- **research-engineer**: Research engineering specialist for building scalable research infrastructure, experiment management, distributed computing, and reproducible research systems. Invoked for research platforms, large-scale experiments, compute optimization, and bridging research-to-production gaps. ([shivanshu814/TurboSupra-Agents](https://github.com/shivanshu814/TurboSupra-Agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-engineer.md))
- **research-evidence-agent**: Live external-data-source feasibility researcher. Invoked by the Planner during the Research & Design phase to test real external APIs (PubMed, ClinicalTrials.gov, DSLD, openFDA, DataForSEO, etc.) and confirm exactly what real, structured data they expose before any code is designed around them. Never trusts documentation alone — makes real live calls. Does not implement. ([elchananteitzwork-a11y/supplement-intelligence](https://github.com/elchananteitzwork-a11y/supplement-intelligence/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-evidence-agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **research-evidence-bureau**: Read-only research and evidence agent. Use whenever a task requires verifying an external standard (49 CFR, TDG, NIST SP, OWASP, ISO 27001, vendor documentation), comparing claims to sources, building a research dossier, or detecting source contradictions before code is written. Produces citation-bearing documents, never product code. ([A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E](https://github.com/A-C-I-SOFTWARE-AND-DEVELOPMENT/M.U.S.E/blob/HEAD/recovered-agent-sources/from-hazmat-command/agents/research-evidence-bureau.md) · ★3)
- **research-execution-and-synthesis-specialist**: RUNNING & SYNTHESIZING UX research — protocol & task design, non-leading moderation, usability testing (success + severity), survey design (no leading/double-barreled items), and synthesis (affinity/thematic analysis, observation vs interpretation, bias-guarding). NOT roadmap → product-management. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/ux-research/agents/research-execution-and-synthesis-specialist.md) · ★7)
- **research-executor**: Executes data acquisition, cleaning, transformation, and visualization tasks with atomic precision. Spawned by orchestrator for Stages 5-8 operations. Each invocation performs exactly ONE operation with pre/post validation. ([brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/blob/HEAD/skills/17-DAAF-Contribution-Community-daaf/dot-claude/agents/research-executor.md) · ★3.9k · 3 copies)
- **research-executor**: Drives the research phase (prompts/01_research_prompt.md). Produces the project's knowledge directory (default kb/) of authoritative, cited domain knowledge. Reads STATE.md to resume; follows hard task gates with one retry; updates STATE.md on every gate evaluation. ([lhyman1701/gated-orchestration-template](https://github.com/lhyman1701/gated-orchestration-template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/research-executor.md))
- **research-exemplar**: Read `references/research.md` for the full stage playbook. ([WUBING2023/PaperSpine](https://github.com/WUBING2023/PaperSpine/blob/HEAD/dist/claude/skills/paper-spine/agents/research-exemplar.md) · ★5.5k · 8 copies)
- **research-expander**: Task-specific research subagent for the prd-taskmaster expand-tasks skill. Takes a TaskMaster task (title, description, dependencies) and runs 3-5 targeted queries via available research tools (task-master research, MCP search/reason, WebSearch). Returns structured summary (~25-40 lines) with citations suitable for writing back to tasks.json via write-research. Delegate here after Phase 4 (Report) produces a draft, before Phase 5 (Reflection) is treated as complete. ([dwarvesf/dwarves-kit](https://github.com/dwarvesf/dwarves-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/research-features.md) · ★11)

Dispatch whenever a tech-research run is about to lock in "these are the options" and "this is the question" without an outside check on either. ([cjsteigerwald/ai-crew](https://github.com/cjsteigerwald/ai-crew/blob/HEAD/tech-research/agents/research-framing-challenger.md))

- **research-ideator**: Research ideation specialist for VLM/LLM papers. Use when starting a new project, pivoting after negative results, or expanding a kernel into a paper-worthy portfolio. - **research-internal-sweep**: Dispatch first, before any external gathering, whenever a tech-research question needs to know what this organization already decided, tried, or observed. Owns T0 (internal) sources — the local `&lt;research_root&gt;` tree only, so coverage on this machine is structurally partial. Always run this lane before tech-research:research-vendor-docs, tech-research:research-code-and-issues, or tech-research:research-operator-experience, since a prior internal decision can make external gathering unnecessary or reframe it. ([cjsteigerwald/ai-crew](https://github.com/cjsteigerwald/ai-crew/blob/HEAD/tech-research/agents/research-internal-sweep.md))
- **research-investigation-worker**: Execution environment for the `research-investigation` skill when it runs as a forked subagent (via `context: fork`). The skill body — supplied as this subagent's prompt — drives the entire investigation including the inline web search-fetch-verify loop. This file only declares the tool surface and enforces the no-commit rule. Spawned by `/research-investigation-cycle` (one fork per directive in a batch) or directly by a human invoking `/research-investigation`. ([mtrense/skills](https://github.com/mtrense/skills/blob/HEAD/research/agents/research-investigation-worker.md) · ★3)
- **research-investigator**: Deep investigation agent for bishx-research. Researches assigned sub-topics with embedded skepticism, source verification, and multi-tier confidence tagging. ([bish-x/claude-bishx](https://github.com/bish-x/claude-bishx/blob/HEAD/agents/research-investigator.md) · ★34)
- **research-investigator**: Methodical multi-source research that builds an evidence-vetted case from primary sources. Triangulates independent corroboration, runs adversarial and falsifiability checks, surfaces premise problems, and produces structured reports with inline epistemic labels and ACM citations. Pair with `research-analyst` (Opus) for judgment-led synthesis or cross-source pattern recognition.
